   456→//   - ACTIVE → condense immediately (each commit gets its own checkpoint)
   457→//   - IDLE → condense immediately
   458→//   - ENDED → condense if files touched, discard if empty
   459→//
   460→// After condensation for ACTIVE sessions, remaining uncommitted files are
   461→// carried forward to a new shadow branch so the next commit gets its own checkpoint.
   462→//
   463→// Shadow branches are only deleted when ALL sessions sharing the branch are non-active
   464→// and were condensed during this PostCommit.
   465→
   466→// postCommitActionHandler implements session.ActionHandler for PostCommit.
   467→// Each session in the loop gets its own handler with per-session context.
   468→// Handler methods use the *State parameter from ApplyTransition (same pointer
   469→// as the state being transitioned) rather than capturing state separately.
   470→type postCommitActionHandler struct {
   471→	s                      *ManualCommitStrategy
   472→	logCtx                 context.Context
   473→	repo                   *git.Repository
   474→	checkpointID           id.CheckpointID
   475→	head                   *plumbing.Reference
   476→	commit                 *object.Commit
   477→	newHead                string
   478→	shadowBranchName       string
   479→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{}
   480→	committedFileSet       map[string]struct{}
   481→	hasNew                 bool
   482→
   483→	// Output: set by handler methods, read by caller after TransitionAndLog.
   484→	condensed bool
   485→}
   486→
   487→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondense(state *session.State) error {
   488→	// For ACTIVE sessions, any commit during the turn is session-related.
   489→	// For IDLE/ENDED sessions (e.g., carry-forward), also require that the
   490→	// committed files overlap with the session's remaining files AND have
   491→	// matching content — otherwise an unrelated commit (or a commit with
   492→	// completely replaced content) would incorrectly get this session's checkpoint.
   493→	shouldCondense := h.hasNew
   494→	if shouldCondense && !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   495→		shouldCondense = filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, state.FilesTouched)
   496→	}
   497→
   498→	logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: HandleCondense decision",
   499→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   500→		slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   501→		slog.Bool("has_new", h.hasNew),
   502→		slog.Bool("should_condense", shouldCondense),
   503→		slog.String("shadow_branch", h.shadowBranchName),
   504→	)
   505→
   506→	if shouldCondense {
   507→		h.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)
   508→	} else {
   509→		h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   510→	}
   511→	return nil
   512→}
   513→
   514→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(state *session.State) error {
   515→	shouldCondense := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.hasNew
   516→
   517→	logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched decision",
   518→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   519→		slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   520→		slog.Bool("has_new", h.hasNew),
   521→		slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
   522→		slog.Bool("should_condense", shouldCondense),
   523→		slog.String("shadow_branch", h.shadowBranchName),
   524→	)
   525→
   526→	if shouldCondense {
   527→		h.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)
   528→	} else {
   529→		h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   530→	}
   531→	return nil
   532→}
   533→
   534→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleDiscardIfNoFiles(state *session.State) error {
   535→	if len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   536→		logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)",
   537→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   538→		)
   539→	}
   540→	h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   541→	return nil
   542→}
   543→
   544→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleWarnStaleSession(_ *session.State) error {
   545→	// Not produced by EventGitCommit; no-op for exhaustiveness.
   546→	return nil
   547→}
   548→
   549→// During rebase/cherry-pick/revert operations, phase transitions are skipped entirely.
   550→//
   551→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   552→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   553→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   554→
   555→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   556→	if err != nil {
   557→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   558→	}
   559→
   560→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   561→	head, err := repo.Head()
   562→	if err != nil {
   563→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   567→	if err != nil {
   568→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   569→	}
   570→
   571→	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   572→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
   573→	if !found {
   574→		// No trailer — user removed it or it was never added (mid-turn commit).
   575→		// Still update BaseCommit for active sessions so future commits can match.
   576→		s.postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx, head)
   577→		return nil
   578→	}
   579→
   580→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   581→	if err != nil {
   582→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   583→	}
   584→
   585→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   586→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   587→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   588→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
   589→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   590→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   591→		)
   592→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   593→	}
   594→
   595→	// Build transition context
   596→	isRebase := isGitSequenceOperation()
   597→	transitionCtx := session.TransitionContext{
   598→		IsRebaseInProgress: isRebase,
   599→	}
   600→
   601→	if isRebase {
   602→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: rebase/sequence in progress, skipping phase transitions",
   603→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   604→		)
   605→	}
   606→
   607→	// Track shadow branch names and whether they can be deleted
   608→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   609→	// Track active sessions that were NOT condensed — their shadow branches must be preserved
   610→	uncondensedActiveOnBranch := make(map[string]bool)
   611→
   612→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   613→	committedFileSet := filesChangedInCommit(commit)
   614→
   615→	for _, state := range sessions {
   616→		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   617→
   618→		// Check for new content (needed for TransitionContext and condensation).
   619→		// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content exists so we
   620→		// don't silently skip data that should have been condensed.
   621→		//
   622→		// For ACTIVE sessions: the commit has a checkpoint trailer (verified above),
   623→		// meaning PrepareCommitMsg already determined this commit is session-related.
   624→		// The trailer is only added when either:
   625→		//   - No TTY (agent/subagent committing) — added unconditionally
   626→		//   - TTY (human committing) — added after content detection confirmed agent work
   627→		// In both cases, PrepareCommitMsg already validated this commit. We trust
   628→		// that decision here. Transcript-based re-validation is unreliable because
   629→		// subagent transcripts may not be available yet (subagent still running).
   630→		var hasNew bool
   631→		if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   632→			hasNew = true
   633→		} else {
   634→			var contentErr error
   635→			hasNew, contentErr = s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   636→			if contentErr != nil {
   637→				hasNew = true
   638→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: error checking session content, assuming new content",
   639→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   640→					slog.String("error", contentErr.Error()),
   641→				)
   642→			}
   643→		}
   644→		transitionCtx.HasFilesTouched = len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
   645→
   646→		// Save FilesTouched BEFORE TransitionAndLog — the handler's condensation
   647→		// clears it, but we need the original list for carry-forward computation.
   648→		// For mid-session commits (ACTIVE, no shadow branch), state.FilesTouched may be empty
   649→		// because no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet. Extract files from transcript.
   650→		filesTouchedBefore := make([]string, len(state.FilesTouched))
   651→		copy(filesTouchedBefore, state.FilesTouched)
   652→		if len(filesTouchedBefore) == 0 && state.Phase.IsActive() && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
   653→			filesTouchedBefore = s.extractFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)
   654→		}
   655→
   656→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward prep",
   657→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   658→			slog.Bool("is_active", state.Phase.IsActive()),
   659→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
   660→			slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   661→			slog.Any("files", filesTouchedBefore),
   662→		)
   663→
   664→		// Run the state machine transition with handler for strategy-specific actions.
   665→		handler := &postCommitActionHandler{
   666→			s:                      s,
   667→			logCtx:                 logCtx,
   668→			repo:                   repo,
   669→			checkpointID:           checkpointID,
   670→			head:                   head,
   671→			commit:                 commit,
   672→			newHead:                newHead,
   673→			shadowBranchName:       shadowBranchName,
   674→			shadowBranchesToDelete: shadowBranchesToDelete,
   675→			committedFileSet:       committedFileSet,
   676→			hasNew:                 hasNew,
   677→		}
   678→
   679→		if err := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventGitCommit, transitionCtx, handler); err != nil {
   680→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: post-commit action handler error: %v\n", err)
   681→		}
   682→
   683→		// Record checkpoint ID for ACTIVE sessions so HandleTurnEnd can finalize
   684→		// with full transcript. IDLE/ENDED sessions already have complete transcripts.
   685→		// NOTE: This check runs AFTER TransitionAndLog updated the phase. It relies on
   686→		// ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE (phase stays ACTIVE). If that state machine
   687→		// transition ever changed, this guard would silently stop recording IDs.
   688→		if handler.condensed && state.Phase.IsActive() {
   689→			state.TurnCheckpointIDs = append(state.TurnCheckpointIDs, checkpointID.String())
   690→		}
   691→
   692→		// Carry forward remaining uncommitted files so the next commit gets its
   693→		// own checkpoint ID. This applies to ALL phases — if a user splits their
   694→		// commit across two `git commit` invocations, each gets a 1:1 checkpoint.
   695→		// Uses content-aware comparison: if user did `git add -p` and committed
   696→		// partial changes, the file still has remaining agent changes to carry forward.
   697→		if handler.condensed {
   698→			remainingFiles := filesWithRemainingAgentChanges(repo, shadowBranchName, commit, filesTouchedBefore, committedFileSet)
   699→			state.FilesTouched = remainingFiles
   700→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward decision (content-aware)",
   701→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   702→				slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   703→				slog.Int("committed_files", len(committedFileSet)),
   704→				slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
   705→				slog.Any("remaining", remainingFiles),
   706→				slog.Any("committed_files", committedFileSet),
   707→			)
   708→			if len(remainingFiles) > 0 {
   709→				s.carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(logCtx, repo, state, remainingFiles)
   710→			}
   711→		}
   712→
   713→		// Save the updated state
   714→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   715→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   716→		}
   717→
   718→		// Only preserve shadow branch for active sessions that were NOT condensed.
   719→		// Condensed sessions already have their data on entire/checkpoints/v1.
   720→		if state.Phase.IsActive() && !handler.condensed {
   721→			uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
   722→		}
   723→	}
   724→
   725→	// Clean up shadow branches — only delete when ALL sessions on the branch are non-active
   726→	// or were condensed during this PostCommit.
   727→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
   728→		if uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {
   729→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: preserving shadow branch (active session exists)",
   730→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   731→			)
   732→			continue
   733→		}
   734→		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   735→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
   736→		} else {
   737→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   738→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
   739→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   740→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   741→			)
   742→		}
   743→	}
   744→
   745→	return nil
   746→}
   747→
   748→// condenseAndUpdateState runs condensation for a session and updates state afterward.
   749→// Returns true if condensation succeeded.
   750→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
   751→	logCtx context.Context,
   752→	repo *git.Repository,
   753→	checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
   754→	state *SessionState,
   755→	head *plumbing.Reference,
   756→	shadowBranchName string,
   757→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{},
   758→	committedFiles map[string]struct{},
   759→) bool {
   760→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, committedFiles)
   761→	if err != nil {
   762→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
   763→			state.SessionID, err)
   764→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: condensation failed",
   765→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   766→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   767→		)
   768→		return false
   769→	}
   770→
   771→	// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
   772→	shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}
   773→
   774→	// Update session state for the new base commit
   775→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   776→	state.BaseCommit = newHead
   777→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = newHead
   778→	state.StepCount = 0
   779→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   780→
   781→	// Clear attribution tracking — condensation already used these values
   782→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   783→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   784→	state.FilesTouched = nil
   785→
   786→	// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits can reuse it (e.g., amend restores trailer)
   787→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   788→
   789→	shortID := state.SessionID
   790→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   791→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   792→	}
   793→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
   794→		shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)
   795→
   796→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session condensed",
   797→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   798→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   799→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   800→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", result.TotalTranscriptLines),
   801→	)
   802→
   803→	return true
   804→}
   805→
   806→// updateBaseCommitIfChanged updates BaseCommit to newHead if it changed.
   807→// Only updates ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions should NOT have their
   808→// BaseCommit updated, as this would cause them to be incorrectly associated
   809→// with a new shadow branch and potentially condensed on future commits.
   810→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState, newHead string) {
   811→	// Only update ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions are kept around for
   812→	// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   813→	if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   814→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updateBaseCommitIfChanged skipped non-active session",
   815→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   816→			slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   817→		)
   818→		return
   819→	}
   820→	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   821→		state.BaseCommit = newHead
   822→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updated BaseCommit",
   823→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   824→			slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   825→		)
   826→	}
   827→}
   828→
   829→// postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly updates BaseCommit for all sessions on the current
   830→// worktree when a commit has no Entire-Checkpoint trailer. This prevents BaseCommit
   831→// from going stale, which would cause future PrepareCommitMsg calls to skip the
   832→// session (BaseCommit != currentHeadHash filter).
   833→//
   834→// Unlike the full PostCommit flow, this does NOT fire EventGitCommit or trigger
   835→// condensation — it only keeps BaseCommit in sync with HEAD.
   836→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx context.Context, head *plumbing.Reference) {
   837→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   838→	if err != nil {
   839→		return // Silent failure — hooks must be resilient
   840→	}
   841→
   842→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   843→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   844→		return
   845→	}
   846→
   847→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   848→	for _, state := range sessions {
   849→		// Only update active sessions. Idle/ended sessions are kept around for
   850→		// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   851→		if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   852→			continue
   853→		}
   854→		if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   855→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit (no trailer): updating BaseCommit",
   856→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   857→				slog.String("old_base", truncateHash(state.BaseCommit)),
   858→				slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   859→			)
   860→			state.BaseCommit = newHead
   861→			if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   862→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   863→			}
   864→		}
   865→	}
   866→}
   867→
   868→// truncateHash safely truncates a git hash to 7 chars for logging.
   869→func truncateHash(h string) string {
   870→	if len(h) > 7 {
   871→		return h[:7]
   872→	}
   873→	return h
   874→}
   875→
   876→// filterSessionsWithNewContent returns sessions that have new transcript content
   877→// beyond what was already condensed.
   878→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo *git.Repository, sessions []*SessionState) []*SessionState {
   879→	var result []*SessionState
   880→
   881→	for _, state := range sessions {
   882→		hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   883→		if err != nil {
   884→			// On error, include the session (fail open for hooks)
   885→			result = append(result, state)
   886→			continue
   887→		}
   888→		if hasNew {
   889→			result = append(result, state)
   890→		}
   891→	}
   892→
   893→	return result
   894→}
   895→
   896→// sessionHasNewContent checks if a session has new transcript content
   897→// beyond what was already condensed.
   898→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContent(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   899→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "manual-commit")
   900→
   901→	// Get shadow branch
   902→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   903→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   904→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   905→	if err != nil {
   906→		// No shadow branch means no Stop has happened since the last condensation.
   907→		// However, the agent may have done work (including commits) without a Stop.
   908→		// Check the live transcript to detect this scenario.
   909→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no shadow branch, checking live transcript",
   910→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   911→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   912→		)
   913→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   914→	}
   915→
   916→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   917→	if err != nil {
   918→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   919→	}
   920→
   921→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   922→	if err != nil {
   923→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   924→	}
   925→
   926→	// Look for transcript file
   927→	metadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + state.SessionID
   928→	var transcriptLines int
   929→	var hasTranscriptFile bool
   930→
   931→	if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   932→		hasTranscriptFile = true
   933→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   934→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, content)
   935→		}
   936→	} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   937→		hasTranscriptFile = true
   938→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   939→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, content)
   940→		}
   941→	}
   942→
   943→	// If shadow branch exists but has no transcript (e.g., carry-forward from mid-session commit),
   944→	// check if the session has FilesTouched. Carry-forward sets FilesTouched with remaining files.
   945→	if !hasTranscriptFile {
   946→		if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   947→			// Shadow branch has files from carry-forward - check if staged files overlap
   948→			// AND have matching content (content-aware check).
   949→			stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
   950→			if len(stagedFiles) > 0 {
   951→				// PrepareCommitMsg context: check staged files overlap with content
   952→				result := stagedFilesOverlapWithContent(repo, tree, stagedFiles, state.FilesTouched)
   953→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no transcript, carry-forward with staged files",
   954→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   955→					slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
   956→					slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
   957→					slog.Bool("result", result),
   958→				)
   959→				return result, nil
   960→			}
   961→			// PostCommit context: no staged files, but we have carry-forward files.
   962→			// Return true and let the caller do the overlap check with committed files.
   963→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no transcript, carry-forward without staged files (post-commit context)",
   964→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   965→				slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
   966→			)
   967→			return true, nil
   968→		}
   969→		// No transcript and no FilesTouched - fall back to live transcript check
   970→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no transcript and no files touched, checking live transcript",
   971→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   972→		)
   973→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   974→	}
   975→
   976→	// Check if there's new content to condense. Two cases:
   977→	// 1. Transcript has grown since last condensation (new prompts/responses)
   978→	// 2. FilesTouched has files not yet committed (carry-forward scenario)
   979→	//
   980→	// For PrepareCommitMsg context, we verify staged files overlap with session's files
   981→	// using content-aware matching to detect reverted files.
   982→	// For PostCommit context, getStagedFiles() is empty (files already committed),
   983→	// so we return true and let the caller do the overlap check via filesOverlapWithContent.
   984→
   985→	hasTranscriptGrowth := transcriptLines > state.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   986→	hasUncommittedFiles := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
   987→
   988→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: transcript check",
   989→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   990→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", transcriptLines),
   991→		slog.Int("checkpoint_transcript_start", state.CheckpointTranscriptStart),
   992→		slog.Bool("has_transcript_growth", hasTranscriptGrowth),
   993→		slog.Bool("has_uncommitted_files", hasUncommittedFiles),
   994→	)
   995→
   996→	if !hasTranscriptGrowth && !hasUncommittedFiles {
   997→		return false, nil // No new content and no carry-forward files
   998→	}
   999→
  1000→	// Check if staged files overlap with session's files with content-aware matching.
  1001→	// This is primarily for PrepareCommitMsg; in PostCommit, stagedFiles is empty.
  1002→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
  1003→	if len(stagedFiles) > 0 {
  1004→		result := stagedFilesOverlapWithContent(repo, tree, stagedFiles, state.FilesTouched)
  1005→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: staged files overlap check",
  1006→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1007→			slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
  1008→			slog.Bool("result", result),
  1009→		)
  1010→		return result, nil
  1011→	}
  1012→
  1013→	// No staged files - either PostCommit context or edge case.
  1014→	// Return transcript growth status. For PostCommit with hasTranscriptFile=true,
  1015→	// if there's no transcript growth, the session hasn't done new work since last checkpoint.
  1016→	// (Carry-forward creates a shadow branch WITHOUT transcript, handled in the block above.)
  1017→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no staged files, returning transcript growth",
  1018→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1019→		slog.Bool("has_transcript_growth", hasTranscriptGrowth),
  1020→		slog.Bool("has_uncommitted_files", hasUncommittedFiles),
  1021→	)
  1022→	return hasTranscriptGrowth, nil
  1023→}
  1024→
  1025→// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content
  1026→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
  1027→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
  1028→//
  1029→// Returns true if:
  1030→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
  1031→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
  1032→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
  1033→//
  1034→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
  1035→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.
  1036→//
  1037→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
  1038→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
  1039→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1040→
  1041→	modifiedFiles, ok := s.extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)
  1042→	if !ok || len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
  1043→		return false, nil
  1044→	}
  1045→
  1046→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: found file modifications",
  1047→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1048→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(modifiedFiles)),
  1049→	)
  1050→
  1051→	// Check if any modified files overlap with currently staged files
  1052→	// This ensures we only add checkpoint trailers to commits that include
  1053→	// files the agent actually modified
  1054→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
  1055→
  1056→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: comparing staged vs modified",
  1057→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1058→		slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
  1059→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(modifiedFiles)),
  1060→	)
  1061→
  1062→	if !hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, modifiedFiles) {
  1063→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: no overlap between staged and modified files",
  1064→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1065→		)
  1066→		return false, nil // No overlap - staged files are unrelated to agent's work
  1067→	}
  1068→
  1069→	return true, nil
  1070→}
  1071→
  1072→// extractFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified file paths from the live transcript.
  1073→// Returns empty slice if extraction fails (fail-open behavior for hooks).
  1074→// Extracts files from the transcript starting at CheckpointTranscriptStart, which gives
  1075→// files touched since the last condensation — used for carry-forward computation.
  1076→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) []string {
  1077→	return s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
  1078→}
  1079→
  1080→// extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified files from the live
  1081→// transcript that are NEW since the last condensation. Returns the normalized file list
  1082→// and whether the extraction succeeded. Used by sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript
  1083→// to detect agent work.
  1084→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) ([]string, bool) {
  1085→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1086→
  1087→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
  1088→		return nil, false
  1089→	}
  1090→
  1091→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
  1092→	if err != nil {
  1093→		return nil, false
  1094→	}
  1095→
  1096→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
  1097→	if !ok {
  1098→		return nil, false
  1099→	}
  1100→
  1101→	// Check if transcript has grown since last condensation
  1102→	currentPos, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(state.TranscriptPath)
  1103→	if err != nil {
  1104→		return nil, false
  1105→	}
  1106→	if currentPos <= state.CheckpointTranscriptStart {
  1107→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: no new content",
  1108→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1109→			slog.Int("current_pos", currentPos),
  1110→			slog.Int("start_offset", state.CheckpointTranscriptStart),
  1111→		)
  1112→		return nil, true // No new content, but extraction succeeded
  1113→	}
  1114→
  1115→	return s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart), true
  1116→}
  1117→
  1118→// extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified files from the live transcript
  1119→// (including subagent transcripts) starting from the given offset, and normalizes them
  1120→// to repo-relative paths. Returns the normalized file list.
  1121→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState, offset int) []string {
  1122→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1123→
  1124→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
  1125→		return nil
  1126→	}
  1127→
  1128→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
  1129→	if err != nil {
  1130→		return nil
  1131→	}
  1132→
  1133→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
  1134→	if !ok {
  1135→		return nil
  1136→	}
  1137→
  1138→	var modifiedFiles []string
  1139→
  1140→	// For Claude Code, use ExtractAllModifiedFiles which parses the main transcript
  1141→	// AND subagent transcripts in a single pass, avoiding redundant parsing.
  1142→	if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode {
  1143→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(state.TranscriptPath), state.SessionID, "subagents")
  1144→		allFiles, extractErr := claudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(state.TranscriptPath, offset, subagentsDir)
  1145→		if extractErr != nil {
  1146→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript: extraction failed",
  1147→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1148→				slog.String("error", extractErr.Error()),
  1149→			)
  1150→		} else {
  1151→			modifiedFiles = allFiles
  1152→		}
  1153→	} else {
  1154→		files, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, offset)
  1155→		if err != nil {
  1156→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript: main transcript extraction failed",
  1157→				slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
  1158→				slog.Any("error", err),
  1159→			)
  1160→		} else {
  1161→			modifiedFiles = files
  1162→		}
  1163→	}
  1164→
  1165→	if len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
  1166→		return nil
  1167→	}
  1168→
  1169→	// Normalize to repo-relative paths.
  1170→	// Transcript tool_use entries contain absolute paths (e.g., /Users/alex/project/src/main.go)
  1171→	// but getStagedFiles/committedFiles use repo-relative paths (e.g., src/main.go).
  1172→	basePath := state.WorktreePath
  1173→	if basePath == "" {
  1174→		if wp, wpErr := GetWorktreePath(); wpErr == nil {
  1175→			basePath = wp
  1176→		}
  1177→	}
  1178→	if basePath != "" {
  1179→		normalized := make([]string, 0, len(modifiedFiles))
  1180→		for _, f := range modifiedFiles {
  1181→			if rel := paths.ToRelativePath(f, basePath); rel != "" {
  1182→				normalized = append(normalized, rel)
  1183→			} else {
  1184→				normalized = append(normalized, f)
  1185→			}
  1186→		}
  1187→		modifiedFiles = normalized
  1188→	}
  1189→
  1190→	return modifiedFiles
  1191→}
  1192→
  1193→// addTrailerForAgentCommit handles the fast path when an agent is committing
  1194→// (ACTIVE session + no TTY). Generates a checkpoint ID and adds the trailer
  1195→// directly, bypassing content detection and interactive prompts.
  1196→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string, state *SessionState, source string) error {
  1197→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
  1198→	if err != nil {
  1199→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1200→	}
  1201→
  1202→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
  1203→	if err != nil {
  1204→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1205→	}
  1206→
  1207→	message := string(content)
  1208→
  1209→	// Don't add if trailer already exists
  1210→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
  1211→		return nil
  1212→	}
  1213→
  1214→	message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
  1215→
  1216→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: agent commit trailer added",
  1217→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
  1218→		slog.String("source", source),
  1219→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
  1220→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1221→	)
  1222→
  1223→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
  1224→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1225→	}
  1226→	return nil
  1227→}
  1228→
  1229→// addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.
  1230→// Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).
  1231→func addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {
  1232→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
  1233→
  1234→	// If message already ends with trailers (lines starting with key:), just append
  1235→	// Otherwise, add a blank line first
  1236→	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimRight(message, "\n"), "\n")
  1237→
  1238→	// Check if the message already ends with a trailer paragraph.
  1239→	// Git trailers must be in a separate paragraph (preceded by a blank line).
  1240→	// A single-paragraph message (e.g., just a subject line) cannot have trailers,
  1241→	// even if the subject contains ": " (like conventional commits: "docs: Add foo").
  1242→	//
  1243→	// Scan from the bottom: find the last paragraph of non-comment content,
  1244→	// then check if it looks like trailers AND has a blank line above it.
  1245→	hasTrailers := false
  1246→	i := len(lines) - 1
  1247→
  1248→	// Skip trailing comment lines
  1249→	for i >= 0 && strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(lines[i]), "#") {
  1250→		i--
  1251→	}
  1252→
  1253→	// Check if the last non-comment line looks like a trailer
  1254→	if i >= 0 {
  1255→		line := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
  1256→		if line != "" && strings.Contains(line, ": ") {
  1257→			// Found a trailer-like line. Now scan upward past the trailer block
  1258→			// to verify there's a blank line (paragraph separator) above it.
  1259→			for i > 0 {
  1260→				i--
  1261→				above := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
  1262→				if strings.HasPrefix(above, "#") {
  1263→					continue
  1264→				}
  1265→				if above == "" {
  1266→					// Blank line found above trailer block — real trailers
  1267→					hasTrailers = true
  1268→					break
  1269→				}
  1270→				if !strings.Contains(above, ": ") {
  1271→					// Non-trailer, non-blank line — this is message body, not trailers
  1272→					break
  1273→				}
  1274→				// Another trailer-like line, keep scanning upward
  1275→			}
  1276→		}
  1277→	}
  1278→
  1279→	if hasTrailers {
  1280→		// Append trailer directly
  1281→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n" + trailer + "\n"
  1282→	}
  1283→
  1284→	// Add blank line before trailer
  1285→	return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n"
  1286→}
  1287→
  1288→// addCheckpointTrailerWithComment adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer with an explanatory comment.
  1289→// The trailer is placed above the git comment block but below the user's message area,
  1290→// with a comment explaining that the user can remove it if they don't want to link the commit
  1291→// to the agent session. If prompt is non-empty, it's shown as context.
  1292→func addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, agentName, prompt string) string {
  1293→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
  1294→	commentLines := []string{
  1295→		"# Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer above if you don't want to link this commit to " + agentName + " session context.",
  1296→	}
  1297→	if prompt != "" {
  1298→		commentLines = append(commentLines, "# Last Prompt: "+prompt)
  1299→	}
  1300→	commentLines = append(commentLines, "# The trailer will be added to your next commit based on this branch.")
  1301→	comment := strings.Join(commentLines, "\n")
  1302→
  1303→	lines := strings.Split(message, "\n")
  1304→
  1305→	// Find where the git comment block starts (first # line)
  1306→	commentStart := -1
  1307→	for i, line := range lines {
  1308→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
  1309→			commentStart = i
  1310→			break
  1311→		}
  1312→	}
  1313→
  1314→	if commentStart == -1 {
  1315→		// No git comments, append trailer at the end
  1316→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n"
  1317→	}
  1318→
  1319→	// Split into user content and git comments
  1320→	userContent := strings.Join(lines[:commentStart], "\n")
  1321→	gitComments := strings.Join(lines[commentStart:], "\n")
  1322→
  1323→	// Build result: user content, blank line, trailer, comment, blank line, git comments
  1324→	userContent = strings.TrimRight(userContent, "\n")
  1325→	if userContent == "" {
  1326→		// No user content yet - leave space for them to type, then trailer
  1327→		// Two newlines: first for user's message line, second for blank separator
  1328→		return "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
  1329→	}
  1330→	return userContent + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
  1331→}
  1332→
  1333→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session or updates an existing one.
  1334→// This implements the optional SessionInitializer interface.
  1335→// Called during UserPromptSubmit to allow git hooks to detect active sessions.
  1336→//
  1337→// If the session already exists and HEAD has moved (e.g., user committed), updates
  1338→// BaseCommit to the new HEAD so future checkpoints go to the correct shadow branch.
  1339→//
  1340→// If there's an existing shadow branch with commits from a different session ID,
  1341→// returns a SessionIDConflictError to prevent orphaning existing session work.
  1342→//
  1343→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
  1344→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
  1345→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
  1346→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error {
  1347→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1348→	if err != nil {
  1349→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
  1350→	}
  1351→
  1352→	// Check if session already exists
  1353→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1354→	if err != nil {
  1355→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check session state: %w", err)
  1356→	}
  1357→
  1358→	if state != nil && state.BaseCommit != "" {
  1359→		// Session is fully initialized — apply phase transition for TurnStart.
  1360→		if transErr := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
  1361→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: turn start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
  1362→		}
  1363→
  1364→		// Generate a new TurnID for each turn (correlates carry-forward checkpoints)
  1365→		turnID, err := id.Generate()
  1366→		if err != nil {
  1367→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate turn ID: %w", err)
  1368→		}
  1369→		state.TurnID = turnID.String()
  1370→
  1371→		// Backfill AgentType if empty or set to the generic default "Agent"
  1372→		if !isSpecificAgentType(state.AgentType) && agentType != "" {
  1373→			state.AgentType = agentType
  1374→		}
  1375→
  1376→		// Backfill FirstPrompt if empty (for sessions created before the first_prompt field was added)
  1377→		if state.FirstPrompt == "" && userPrompt != "" {
  1378→			state.FirstPrompt = truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt)
  1379→		}
  1380→
  1381→		// Update transcript path if provided (may change on session resume)
  1382→		if transcriptPath != "" && state.TranscriptPath != transcriptPath {
  1383→			state.TranscriptPath = transcriptPath
  1384→		}
  1385→
  1386→		// Clear checkpoint IDs on every new prompt.
  1387→		// LastCheckpointID is set during PostCommit, cleared at new prompt.
  1388→		// TurnCheckpointIDs tracks mid-turn checkpoints for stop-time finalization.
  1389→		state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1390→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1391→
  1392→		// Calculate attribution at prompt start (BEFORE agent makes any changes)
  1393→		// This captures user edits since the last checkpoint (or base commit for first prompt).
  1394→		// IMPORTANT: Always calculate attribution, even for the first checkpoint, to capture
  1395→		// user edits made before the first prompt. The inner CalculatePromptAttribution handles
  1396→		// nil lastCheckpointTree by falling back to baseTree.
  1397→		promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1398→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1399→
  1400→		// Check if HEAD has moved (user pulled/rebased or committed)
  1401→		// migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded handles renaming the shadow branch and updating state.BaseCommit
  1402→		if _, err := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
  1403→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to check/migrate shadow branch: %w", err)
  1404→		}
  1405→
  1406→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1407→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", err)
  1408→		}
  1409→		return nil
  1410→	}
  1411→	// If state exists but BaseCommit is empty, it's a partial state from concurrent warning
  1412→	// Continue below to properly initialize it
  1413→
  1414→	// Initialize new session
  1415→	state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, transcriptPath, userPrompt)
  1416→	if err != nil {
  1417→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
  1418→	}
  1419→
  1420→	// Apply phase transition: new session starts as ACTIVE.
  1421→	if transErr := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
  1422→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: turn start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
  1423→	}
  1424→
  1425→	// Calculate attribution for pre-prompt edits
  1426→	// This captures any user edits made before the first prompt
  1427→	promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1428→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1429→	if err = s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1430→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save attribution: %w", err)
  1431→	}
  1432→
  1433→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Initialized shadow session: %s\n", sessionID)
  1434→	return nil
  1435→}
  1436→
  1437→// calculatePromptAttributionAtStart calculates attribution at prompt start (before agent runs).
  1438→// This captures user changes since the last checkpoint - no filtering needed since
  1439→// the agent hasn't made any changes yet.
  1440→//
  1441→// IMPORTANT: This reads from the worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1442→// captures in checkpoints. If we read staged content but checkpoints capture worktree content,
  1443→// unstaged changes would be in the checkpoint but not counted in PromptAttribution, causing
  1444→// them to be incorrectly attributed to the agent later.
  1445→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(
  1446→	repo *git.Repository,
  1447→	state *SessionState,
  1448→) PromptAttribution {
  1449→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
  1450→	nextCheckpointNum := state.StepCount + 1
  1451→	result := PromptAttribution{CheckpointNumber: nextCheckpointNum}
  1452→
  1453→	// Get last checkpoint tree from shadow branch (if it exists)
  1454→	// For the first checkpoint, no shadow branch exists yet - this is fine,
  1455→	// CalculatePromptAttribution will use baseTree as the reference instead.
  1456→	var lastCheckpointTree *object.Tree
  1457→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1458→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1459→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1460→	if err != nil {
  1461→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: no shadow branch yet (first checkpoint)",
  1462→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName))
  1463→		// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1464→	} else {
  1465→		shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1466→		if err != nil {
  1467→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow commit",
  1468→				slog.String("shadow_ref", ref.Hash().String()),
  1469→				slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1470→			// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1471→		} else {
  1472→			lastCheckpointTree, err = shadowCommit.Tree()
  1473→			if err != nil {
  1474→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow tree",
  1475→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1476→				// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1477→			}
  1478→		}
  1479→	}
  1480→
  1481→	// Get base tree for agent lines calculation
  1482→	var baseTree *object.Tree
  1483→	if baseCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(state.BaseCommit)); err == nil {
  1484→		if tree, treeErr := baseCommit.Tree(); treeErr == nil {
  1485→			baseTree = tree
  1486→		} else {
  1487→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base tree unavailable",
  1488→				slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
  1489→		}
  1490→	} else {
  1491→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base commit unavailable",
  1492→			slog.String("base_commit", state.BaseCommit),
  1493→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1494→	}
  1495→
  1496→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1497→	if err != nil {
  1498→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree",
  1499→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1500→		return result
  1501→	}
  1502→
  1503→	// Get worktree status to find ALL changed files
  1504→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1505→	if err != nil {
  1506→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree status",
  1507→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1508→		return result
  1509→	}
  1510→
  1511→	worktreeRoot := worktree.Filesystem.Root()
  1512→
  1513→	// Build map of changed files with their worktree content
  1514→	// IMPORTANT: We read from worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1515→	// captures in checkpoints. This ensures attribution is consistent.
  1516→	changedFiles := make(map[string]string)
  1517→	for filePath, fileStatus := range status {
  1518→		// Skip unmodified files
  1519→		if fileStatus.Worktree == git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging == git.Unmodified {
  1520→			continue
  1521→		}
  1522→		// Skip .entire metadata directory (session data, not user code)
  1523→		if strings.HasPrefix(filePath, paths.EntireMetadataDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(filePath, ".entire/") {
  1524→			continue
  1525→		}
  1526→
  1527→		// Always read from worktree to match checkpoint behavior
  1528→		fullPath := filepath.Join(worktreeRoot, filePath)
  1529→		var content string
  1530→		if data, err := os.ReadFile(fullPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // filePath is from git worktree status
  1531→			// Use git's binary detection algorithm (matches getFileContent behavior).
  1532→			// Binary files are excluded from line-based attribution calculations.
  1533→			isBinary, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(data))
  1534→			if binErr == nil && !isBinary {
  1535→				content = string(data)
  1536→			}
  1537→		}
  1538→		// else: file deleted, unreadable, or binary - content remains empty string
  1539→
  1540→		changedFiles[filePath] = content
  1541→	}
  1542→
  1543→	// Use CalculatePromptAttribution from manual_commit_attribution.go
  1544→	result = CalculatePromptAttribution(baseTree, lastCheckpointTree, changedFiles, nextCheckpointNum)
  1545→
  1546→	return result
  1547→}
  1548→
  1549→// getStagedFiles returns a list of files staged for commit.
  1550→func getStagedFiles(repo *git.Repository) []string {
  1551→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1552→	if err != nil {
  1553→		return nil
  1554→	}
  1555→
  1556→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1557→	if err != nil {
  1558→		return nil
  1559→	}
  1560→
  1561→	var staged []string
  1562→	for path, fileStatus := range status {
  1563→		// Check if file is staged (in index)
  1564→		if fileStatus.Staging != git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging != git.Untracked {
  1565→			staged = append(staged, path)
  1566→		}
  1567→	}
  1568→	return staged
  1569→}
  1570→
  1571→// getLastPrompt retrieves the most recent user prompt from a session's shadow branch.
  1572→// Returns empty string if no prompt can be retrieved.
  1573→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getLastPrompt(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) string {
  1574→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1575→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1576→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1577→	if err != nil {
  1578→		return ""
  1579→	}
  1580→
  1581→	// Extract session data to get prompts for commit message generation
  1582→	// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini)
  1583→	// Pass 0 for checkpointTranscriptStart since we're extracting all prompts, not calculating token usage
  1584→	sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, nil, state.AgentType, "", 0)
  1585→	if err != nil || len(sessionData.Prompts) == 0 {
  1586→		return ""
  1587→	}
  1588→
  1589→	// Return the last prompt (most recent work before commit)
  1590→	return sessionData.Prompts[len(sessionData.Prompts)-1]
  1591→}
  1592→
  1593→// HandleTurnEnd dispatches strategy-specific actions emitted when an agent turn ends.
  1594→// The primary job is to finalize all checkpoints from this turn with the full transcript.
  1595→//
  1596→// During a turn, PostCommit writes provisional transcript data (whatever was available
  1597→// at commit time). HandleTurnEnd replaces that with the complete session transcript
  1598→// (from prompt to stop event), ensuring every checkpoint has the full context.
  1599→//
  1600→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
  1601→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HandleTurnEnd(state *SessionState) error {
  1602→	// Finalize all checkpoints from this turn with the full transcript.
  1603→	//
  1604→	// IMPORTANT: This is best-effort - errors are logged but don't fail the hook.
  1605→	// Failing here would prevent session cleanup and could leave state inconsistent.
  1606→	// The provisional transcript from PostCommit is already persisted, so the
  1607→	// checkpoint isn't lost - it just won't have the complete transcript.
  1608→	errCount := s.finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints(state)
  1609→	if errCount > 0 {
  1610→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1611→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "HandleTurnEnd completed with errors (best-effort)",
  1612→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1613→			slog.Int("error_count", errCount),
  1614→		)
  1615→	}
  1616→	return nil
  1617→}
  1618→
  1619→// finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints replaces the provisional transcript in each checkpoint
  1620→// created during this turn with the full session transcript.
  1621→//
  1622→// This is called at turn end (stop hook). During the turn, PostCommit wrote whatever
  1623→// transcript was available at commit time. Now we have the complete transcript and
  1624→// replace it so every checkpoint has the full prompt-to-stop context.
  1625→//
  1626→// Returns the number of errors encountered (best-effort: continues processing on error).
  1627→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints(state *SessionState) int {
  1628→	if len(state.TurnCheckpointIDs) == 0 {
  1629→		return 0 // No mid-turn commits to finalize
  1630→	}
  1631→
  1632→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1633→
  1634→	logging.Info(logCtx, "finalizing turn checkpoints with full transcript",
  1635→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1636→		slog.Int("checkpoint_count", len(state.TurnCheckpointIDs)),
  1637→	)
  1638→
  1639→	errCount := 0
  1640→
  1641→	// Read full transcript from live transcript file
  1642→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
  1643→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: no transcript path, skipping",
  1644→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1645→		)
  1646→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1647→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1648→	}
  1649→
  1650→	fullTranscript, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
  1651→	if err != nil || len(fullTranscript) == 0 {
  1652→		msg := "finalize: empty transcript, skipping"
  1653→		if err != nil {
  1654→			msg = "finalize: failed to read transcript, skipping"
  1655→		}
  1656→		logging.Warn(logCtx, msg,
  1657→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1658→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
  1659→			slog.Any("error", err),
  1660→		)
  1661→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1662→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1663→	}
  1664→
  1665→	// Extract prompts and context from the full transcript
  1666→	prompts := extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, string(fullTranscript))
  1667→	contextBytes := generateContextFromPrompts(prompts)
  1668→
  1669→	// Redact secrets before writing — matches WriteCommitted behavior.
  1670→	// The live transcript on disk contains raw content; redaction must happen
  1671→	// before anything is persisted to the metadata branch.
  1672→	fullTranscript, err = redact.JSONLBytes(fullTranscript)
  1673→	if err != nil {
  1674→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: transcript redaction failed, skipping",
  1675→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1676→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1677→		)
  1678→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1679→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1680→	}
  1681→	for i, p := range prompts {
  1682→		prompts[i] = redact.String(p)
  1683→	}
  1684→	contextBytes = redact.Bytes(contextBytes)
  1685→
  1686→	// Open repository and create checkpoint store
  1687→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1688→	if err != nil {
  1689→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: failed to open repository",
  1690→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1691→		)
  1692→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1693→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1694→	}
  1695→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1696→
  1697→	// Update each checkpoint with the full transcript
  1698→	for _, cpIDStr := range state.TurnCheckpointIDs {
  1699→		cpID, parseErr := id.NewCheckpointID(cpIDStr)
  1700→		if parseErr != nil {
  1701→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: invalid checkpoint ID, skipping",
  1702→				slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1703→				slog.String("error", parseErr.Error()),
  1704→			)
  1705→			errCount++
  1706→			continue
  1707→		}
  1708→
  1709→		updateErr := store.UpdateCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.UpdateCommittedOptions{
  1710→			CheckpointID: cpID,
  1711→			SessionID:    state.SessionID,
  1712→			Transcript:   fullTranscript,
  1713→			Prompts:      prompts,
  1714→			Context:      contextBytes,
  1715→			Agent:        state.AgentType,
  1716→		})
  1717→		if updateErr != nil {
  1718→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: failed to update checkpoint",
  1719→				slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1720→				slog.String("error", updateErr.Error()),
  1721→			)
  1722→			errCount++
  1723→			continue
  1724→		}
  1725→
  1726→		logging.Info(logCtx, "finalize: checkpoint updated with full transcript",
  1727→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1728→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1729→		)
  1730→	}
  1731→
  1732→	// Clear turn checkpoint IDs. Do NOT update CheckpointTranscriptStart here — it was
  1733→	// already set correctly by PostCommit: condenseAndUpdateState sets it to the total
  1734→	// transcript lines when condensing, and carryForwardToNewShadowBranch resets it to 0
  1735→	// when carry-forward is active. Overwriting here would break carry-forward by making
  1736→	// sessionHasNewContent think the transcript is fully consumed (no growth).
  1737→	state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1738→
  1739→	return errCount
  1740→}
  1741→
  1742→// filesChangedInCommit returns the set of files changed in a commit by diffing against its parent.
  1743→func filesChangedInCommit(commit *object.Commit) map[string]struct{} {
  1744→	result := make(map[string]struct{})
  1745→
  1746→	commitTree, err := commit.Tree()
  1747→	if err != nil {
  1748→		return result
  1749→	}
  1750→
  1751→	var parentTree *object.Tree
  1752→	if commit.NumParents() > 0 {
  1753→		parent, err := commit.Parent(0)
  1754→		if err != nil {
  1755→			return result
  1756→		}
  1757→		parentTree, err = parent.Tree()
  1758→		if err != nil {
  1759→			return result
  1760→		}
  1761→	}
  1762→
  1763→	if parentTree == nil {
  1764→		// Initial commit — all files are new
  1765→		if iterErr := commitTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
  1766→			result[f.Name] = struct{}{}
  1767→			return nil
  1768→		}); iterErr != nil {
  1769→			return result
  1770→		}
  1771→		return result
  1772→	}
  1773→
  1774→	changes, err := parentTree.Diff(commitTree)
  1775→	if err != nil {
  1776→		return result
  1777→	}
  1778→	for _, change := range changes {
  1779→		name := change.To.Name
  1780→		if name == "" {
  1781→			name = change.From.Name
  1782→		}
  1783→		result[name] = struct{}{}
  1784→	}
  1785→	return result
  1786→}
  1787→
  1788→// subtractFiles returns files that are NOT in the exclude set.
  1789→func subtractFiles(files []string, exclude map[string]struct{}) []string {
  1790→	var remaining []string
  1791→	for _, f := range files {
  1792→		if _, excluded := exclude[f]; !excluded {
  1793→			remaining = append(remaining, f)
  1794→		}
  1795→	}
  1796→	return remaining
  1797→}
  1798→
  1799→// carryForwardToNewShadowBranch creates a new shadow branch at the current HEAD
  1800→// containing the remaining uncommitted files and all session metadata.
  1801→// This enables the next commit to get its own unique checkpoint.
  1802→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(
  1803→	logCtx context.Context,
  1804→	repo *git.Repository,
  1805→	state *SessionState,
  1806→	remainingFiles []string,
  1807→) {
  1808→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1809→
  1810→	// Don't include metadata directory in carry-forward. The carry-forward branch
  1811→	// only needs to preserve file content for comparison - not the transcript.
  1812→	// Including the transcript would cause sessionHasNewContent to always return true
  1813→	// because CheckpointTranscriptStart is reset to 0 for carry-forward.
  1814→	result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
  1815→		SessionID:         state.SessionID,
  1816→		BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,
  1817→		WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,
  1818→		ModifiedFiles:     remainingFiles,
  1819→		MetadataDir:       "",
  1820→		MetadataDirAbs:    "",
  1821→		CommitMessage:     "carry forward: uncommitted session files",
  1822→		IsFirstCheckpoint: false,
  1823→	})
  1824→	if err != nil {
  1825→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward failed",
  1826→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1827→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1828→		)
  1829→		return
  1830→	}
  1831→	if result.Skipped {
  1832→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward skipped (no changes)",
  1833→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1834→		)
  1835→		return
  1836→	}
  1837→
  1838→	// Update state for the carry-forward checkpoint.
  1839→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0 is intentional: each checkpoint is self-contained with
  1840→	// the full transcript. This trades storage efficiency for simplicity:
  1841→	// - Pro: Each checkpoint is independently readable without needing to stitch together
  1842→	//   multiple checkpoints to understand the session history
  1843→	// - Con: For long sessions with multiple partial commits, each checkpoint includes
  1844→	//   the full transcript, which could be large
  1845→	// An alternative would be incremental checkpoints (only new content since last condensation),
  1846→	// but this would complicate checkpoint retrieval and require careful tracking of dependencies.
  1847→	state.StepCount = 1
  1848→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
  1849→	state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1850→	// NOTE: TurnCheckpointIDs is intentionally NOT cleared here. Those checkpoint
  1851→	// IDs from earlier in the turn still need finalization with the full transcript
  1852→	// when HandleTurnEnd runs at stop time.
  1853→
  1854→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-commit: carried forward remaining files",
  1855→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1856→		slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
  1857→	)
  1858→}
  1859→

> AGENT

Found it! The `prepare-commit-msg` hook is calling `askConfirmTTY()` which tries to read from `/dev/tty` (line 86-87):

```go
reader := bufio.NewReader(tty)
response, err := reader.ReadString('\n')  // <-- HANGS HERE
```

When Gemini runs `git commit -m "..."`, the hook detects `source="message"` and tries to ask the user interactively. But Gemini can't respond.

The hook checks for `ENTIRE_TEST_TTY` env var to skip interactive prompts (line 57-59):

```go
if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
    return defaultYes
}
```

The fix is to set `ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1` in the Gemini runner's environment:

but this is an issue in general then with gemini, right? like if the user tells gemini to commit then he would face this?

> AGENT

Yes, exactly. This is a real bug for Gemini users in general, not just E2E tests. When a user tells Gemini to commit, the `prepare-commit-msg` hook hangs waiting for TTY input that Gemini can't provide.

The fix should be in the hook itself. Looking at the code, the issue is this logic (line 275-281):

```go
// Fast path: when an agent is committing (ACTIVE session + no TTY), skip
if !hasTTY() {
    for _, state := range sessions {
        if state.Phase.IsActive() {
            return s.addTrailerForAgentCommit(...)
        }
    }
}
```

It checks `!hasTTY()` first, but Gemini inherits the user's TTY, so `hasTTY()` returns true and the fast path is skipped.

The fix should be: if there's an ACTIVE session, skip interactive prompts regardless of TTY availability - the agent can't respond either way:

```go
// Fast path: when an agent is committing (ACTIVE session), skip interactive prompts.
// The agent can't respond to TTY prompts even if a TTY is available.
for _, state := range sessions {
    if state.Phase.IsActive() {
        return s.addTrailerForAgentCommit(...)
    }
}
```

Want me to make this fix in `manual_commit_hooks.go`?

Based on the search results, there are several known issues with Gemini CLI hanging when waiting for input. Here's a summary of the relevant findings:

## Known Issues

### 1. Agent Hangs on Interactive Shell Prompts (Issue #10909)
When the Gemini CLI agent runs commands that trigger interactive shell prompts (yes/no, enter a name, overwrite file, etc.), the agent becomes stuck and never progresses.(no content)The agent hangs indefinitely when a command requests interactive input. No fallback, timeout, or guidance is provided. No clear way to resume or supply the needed input.

**Workarounds:**
- Manually aborting with Ctrl+C / Esc.
- Passing -y/--force where supported.

### 2. Subprocess/Non-TTY Hang (Issue #6715)
Gemini CLI version 0.1.22 hangs indefinitely when executed from Node.js child_process (subprocess), while the exact same commands work perfectly when run directly from a terminal.(no content)This regression breaks: Automation tools that spawn Gemini CLI as subprocess, MCP servers using Gemini CLI backend, CI/CD pipelines with Gemini CLI integration, and any programmatic usage of Gemini CLI.

**Workaround:** Close stdin immediately after spawning:
```javascript
child.stdin.end();  // CRITICAL: Close stdin immediately to prevent hang
```

### 3. Windows-Specific Issues (Issue #13549, #14296)
The CLI enters a "zombie" state where it becomes completely unresponsive. It often hangs at "Connecting to MCP servers... (3/4)", waiting indefinitely. When it reaches the input prompt (> Type your message), it refuses to accept any keyboard input.(no content)Running Windows 11 with Gemini 0.18.4, users are getting stuck on "Interactive shell awaiting input... press Ctrl+f to focus shell" - pressing that combo does nothing. So can't use gemini cli due to this.

### 4. CI Environment Detection Issue
The Gemini CLI does not enter interactive mode (no prompt appears) if an environment variable starting with CI_ (e.g., CI_TOKEN) is set. This is because the is-in-ci package, used by the underlying UI framework, detects these variables and assumes a non-interactive CI environment.

**Solution:** If the CI_ prefixed variable is not needed for the CLI to function, you can temporarily unset it for the command. e.g., `env -u CI_TOKEN gemini`

### 5. False Positive "Awaiting Input" (Issue #15835)
During long-running processes, specifically the "preflight" check, the CLI displays the message: "interactive shell awaiting input" However, no user input is actually required. If the process is left alone for approximately 30 seconds, the message disappears and the preflight checks continue as expected.

## Debugging Tips
Use the --debug flag for more detailed output. In interactive mode, press F12 to view the debug console.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.
```

> AGENT

You're right - this is a known Gemini CLI issue. Found the exact bug:

**[Issue #10909: Agent hangs on interactive shell prompts](https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/issues/10909)**

> When the Gemini CLI agent runs commands that trigger interactive shell prompts (yes/no, enter a name, overwrite file, etc.), the agent becomes stuck and never progresses. No fallback, timeout, or guidance is provided.

**Workarounds mentioned:**
- Manually aborting with Ctrl+C / Esc
- Passing `-y`/`--force` where supported

There's also [Issue #6715](https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/issues/6715) about hanging when run as a subprocess - the workaround there is to close stdin immediately.

So the fix options are:

1. **For E2E tests:** Set `ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1` in Gemini's environment (makes our hook skip interactive prompts)

2. **For real users:** This is a Gemini limitation. Our hook could detect it's being called from a Gemini subprocess (check for Gemini-specific env vars?) and skip interactive prompts, but that's a workaround for their bug.

3. **File an issue/feature request** with Gemini CLI asking for better handling of commands that need input

Based on the search results, here's what I found about Gemini CLI's environment variable for detecting subprocesses:

## GEMINI_CLI Environment Variable

When `run_shell_command` executes a command, it sets the `GEMINI_CLI=1` environment variable in the subprocess's environment. This allows scripts or tools to detect if they are being run from within the Gemini CLI.(no content)When a command is executed via `!` or in shell mode, the `GEMINI_CLI=1` environment variable is set in the subprocess's environment. This allows scripts or tools to detect if they are being run from within the Gemini CLI.

### How to Use It

All shell commands executed through Gemini CLI (both via `!` prefix and `run_shell_command` tool) receive the environment variable. This allows scripts and tools to detect they're running within Gemini CLI and adjust behavior accordingly.

### Note on GEMINI_SESSION

The search results did not return any specific information about a `GEMINI_SESSION` environment variable. The documented environment variable for subprocess detection is `GEMINI_CLI=1`, not `GEMINI_SESSION`. It's possible that `GEMINI_SESSION` is not a standard feature, or it may be an internal/undocumented variable.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

> AGENT

Found it. According to the [Gemini CLI Shell Tool docs](https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/shell/):

> When `run_shell_command` executes a command, it sets the **`GEMINI_CLI=1`** environment variable in the subprocess's environment. This allows scripts or tools to detect if they are being run from within the Gemini CLI.

So the fix for `askConfirmTTY` in `manual_commit_hooks.go` should check for `GEMINI_CLI=1`:

```go
func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    // In test mode, don't try to interact with the real TTY
    if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
        return defaultYes
    }
    
    // Gemini CLI sets GEMINI_CLI=1 when running shell commands.
    // The agent can't respond to TTY prompts, so use the default.
    if os.Getenv("GEMINI_CLI") != "" {
        return defaultYes
    }
    
    // ... rest of function
}
```

This way real Gemini users won't experience the hang. Want me to make this fix?

This fix will also help real Gemini users - when Gemini runs `git commit -m "..."`, the hook will detect `GEMINI_CLI=1` and skip the interactive prompt.

> DEVELOPER

do you think the devnull fix is till needed?

> AGENT

Good question. Let me think through what each fix addresses:

1. **`GEMINI_CLI` check** - Fixes the hook hanging when reading from `/dev/tty` during `git commit -m`

2. **`/dev/null` stdin** - Prevents Gemini CLI itself from potentially waiting on stdin

Looking at [Issue #6715](https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/issues/6715) (subprocess hang), the workaround was explicitly closing stdin. So it's probably still a good defensive measure.

But we could test without it to be sure. Want me to remove it and see if the tests still pass? If they do, we can drop it. If they hang again at step 1 (creating files), we know it's needed.

    13→// These tests cover the scenarios documented in docs/architecture/checkpoint-scenarios.md
    14→
    15→// TestE2E_Scenario3_MultipleGranularCommits tests Claude making multiple granular commits
    16→// during a single turn.
    17→//
    18→// Scenario 3: Claude Makes Multiple Granular Commits
    19→// - Claude is instructed to make granular commits
    20→// - Multiple commits happen during one turn
    21→// - Each commit gets its own unique checkpoint ID
    22→// - All checkpoints are finalized together at turn end
    23→func TestE2E_Scenario3_MultipleGranularCommits(t *testing.T) {
    24→	t.Parallel()
    25→
    26→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
    27→
    28→	// Count commits before
    29→	commitsBefore := env.GetCommitCount()
    30→	t.Logf("Commits before: %d", commitsBefore)
    31→
    32→	// Agent creates multiple files and commits them separately
    33→	granularCommitPrompt := `Please do the following tasks, committing after each one:
    34→
    35→1. Create a file called file1.go with this content:
    36→   package main
    37→   func One() int { return 1 }
    38→   Then run: git add file1.go && git commit -m "Add file1"
    39→
    40→2. Create a file called file2.go with this content:
    41→   package main
    42→   func Two() int { return 2 }
    43→   Then run: git add file2.go && git commit -m "Add file2"
    44→
    45→3. Create a file called file3.go with this content:
    46→   package main
    47→   func Three() int { return 3 }
    48→   Then run: git add file3.go && git commit -m "Add file3"
    49→
    50→Do each task in order, making the commit after each file creation.`
    51→
    52→	result, err := env.RunAgentWithTools(granularCommitPrompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})
    53→	require.NoError(t, err)
    54→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
    55→	t.Logf("Agent output: %s", result.Stdout)
    56→
    57→	// Verify all files were created
    58→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("file1.go"), "file1.go should exist")
    59→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("file2.go"), "file2.go should exist")
    60→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("file3.go"), "file3.go should exist")
    61→
    62→	// Verify multiple commits were made
    63→	commitsAfter := env.GetCommitCount()
    64→	t.Logf("Commits after: %d", commitsAfter)
    65→	assert.GreaterOrEqual(t, commitsAfter-commitsBefore, 3, "Should have at least 3 new commits")
    66→
    67→	// Get all checkpoint IDs from history
    68→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
    69→	t.Logf("Found %d checkpoint IDs in commit history", len(checkpointIDs))
    70→	for i, id := range checkpointIDs {
    71→		t.Logf("  Checkpoint %d: %s", i, id)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID (1:1 model)
    75→	if len(checkpointIDs) >= 3 {
    76→		// Verify checkpoint IDs are unique
    77→		idSet := make(map[string]bool)
    78→		for _, id := range checkpointIDs {
    79→			require.Falsef(t, idSet[id], "Checkpoint IDs should be unique: %s is duplicated", id)
    80→			idSet[id] = true
    81→		}
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	// Verify metadata branch exists
    85→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
    86→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
    87→
    88→	// Validate each checkpoint has proper metadata and content
    89→	// Note: checkpointIDs are in reverse chronological order (newest first)
    90→	// So checkpointIDs[0] = file3.go, [1] = file2.go, [2] = file1.go
    91→	//
    92→	// With deferred finalization, all checkpoints from the same turn get the
    93→	// FULL transcript at turn end, so all checkpoints should contain all file names.
    94→	allFiles := []string{"file1.go", "file2.go", "file3.go"}
    95→	for i, cpID := range checkpointIDs {
    96→		fileNum := len(checkpointIDs) - i // Reverse the index to match file numbers
    97→		fileName := fmt.Sprintf("file%d.go", fileNum)
    98→		t.Logf("Validating checkpoint %d: %s (files_touched: %s)", i, cpID, fileName)
    99→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   100→			CheckpointID:              cpID,
   101→			Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   102→			FilesTouched:              []string{fileName},
   103→			ExpectedTranscriptContent: allFiles, // All checkpoints have full transcript
   104→		})
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	// No shadow branches should remain after agent committed all files
   108→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   109→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
   110→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
   111→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
   112→	}
   113→}
